# Containers and Kubernetes

You help developers write, check and explain container images, dev containers and Kubernetes manifests.

## Rules

1. Before you answer, search your knowledge for the subject of the question. Answer from what you find.
2. If your knowledge has nothing on the question, say so in one sentence, then answer from what you know and say that it is not from the knowledge.
3. Name each document you used on a last line that starts with `Sources:`.
4. Give the check command of each file you write: `hadolint` for a Dockerfile, `kubeconform` and `kube-linter` for a manifest. Say that a file is valid only after the user pastes a clean output.
5. Ask what the cluster uses (version, Ingress or Gateway API, Helm or Kustomize) when the answer depends on it.
6. Never give a command that changes a cluster or a registry as if it were a check.
7. Answer in the language of the question, never a mix of two languages. Write files, names and comments in English.
8. You cannot read files or run commands. Give the commands for the user to run, and ask for their output.
9. Never search the web. Never open a URL.

## Answer format

1. The answer in a few short sentences or a short list.
2. The file in a `dockerfile`, `yaml` or `json` block.
3. The check commands.
4. The `Sources:` line.

// Docs server of the knowledge base, for a coding assistant that speaks MCP over stdio.
// Run with Node 22.18 or later, no build step, no package:
//   node adapters/mcp/docs-server.ts [--root <folder>]
// The root holds docs/: the project root by default, or code/ of this repository.
// It speaks the protocol revision 2026-07-28 (request metadata, server/discover) and the
// revisions up to 2025-11-25 (initialize handshake). It never writes a file.
import {
  existsSync,
  readdirSync,
  readFileSync,
  realpathSync,
  statSync,
} from 'node:fs';
import {
  join,
  relative,
  resolve,
  sep,
} from 'node:path';
import { createInterface } from 'node:readline';
import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';
import { parseArgs } from 'node:util';

type Json = Record<string, unknown>;

interface Request {
  id?: number | string | null;
  method?: string;
  params?: Json;
}

interface Entry {
  path: string;
  section?: string;
  text: string;
}

interface ToolResult {
  text: string;
  isError: boolean;
}

export const MODERN_VERSION = '2026-07-28';

export const LEGACY_VERSIONS = [
  '2025-11-25',
  '2025-06-18',
  '2025-03-26',
  '2024-11-05',
];

export const MAX_RESULTS = 10;
export const MAX_CHARS = 16_000;
const VERSION_KEY = 'io.modelcontextprotocol/protocolVersion';
const SERVER_INFO = {
  name: 'knowledge-base-docs',
  version: '0.1.0',
};
const INSTRUCTIONS = 'Find documents of the knowledge base with search_docs. Then read one section with read_doc. Read the table of contents of a document before a section.';
const INDEX_LINE = /^- \[([^\]]+)\]\(((?:docs|library)\/[^)]+)\):\s*(.*)$/;
const DOC_PATH = /^(?:docs|library)\/[\w-]+(?:\/[\w.-]+)*\.md$/;
const cachedEntries = new Map<string, Entry[]>();
const NEWLINE = '\n';
const TOOLS = [
  {
    name: 'search_docs',
    title: 'Search the knowledge base',
    description: 'Search the topic indexes and the section titles of the knowledge base, then the indexes of the official documentation in library/. Returns at most 10 lines, documents of the base first: a document path, or a path and a section title.',
    inputSchema: {
      type: 'object',
      properties: {
        query: {
          type: 'string',
          description: 'English words, for example "valve flow coefficient".',
        },
      },
      required: ['query'],
      additionalProperties: false,
    },
  },
  {
    name: 'read_doc',
    title: 'Read one section of a document',
    description: 'Read one section of a document of the knowledge base. Without a section, returns the summary and the table of contents of the document.',
    inputSchema: {
      type: 'object',
      properties: {
        path: {
          type: 'string',
          description: 'Path of the document, as search_docs gives it, for example "docs/simulation/principles.md".',
        },
        section: {
          type: 'string',
          description: 'Number or title of the section, as the table of contents gives it.',
        },
      },
      required: ['path'],
      additionalProperties: false,
    },
  },
];

export function resolveDocsRoot(root: string): string {
  if (!existsSync(join(root, 'docs')) && existsSync(join(root, 'code', 'docs'))) {
    return join(root, 'code');
  }
  return root;
}

function read(path: string): string {
  return readFileSync(path, 'utf8');
}

// The folder that holds library/: the root in a project, the folder above code/ in this repository.
export function libraryBase(root: string): string {
  return existsSync(join(root, 'library')) || !existsSync(join(root, '..', 'library')) ? root : join(root, '..');
}

function baseOf(root: string, path: string): string {
  return path.startsWith('library/') ? libraryBase(root) : root;
}

function topicIndexes(base: string, folder: 'docs' | 'library'): string[] {
  const top = join(base, folder);

  if (!existsSync(top)) {
    return [];
  }
  return readdirSync(top)
    .filter((topic) => statSync(join(top, topic)).isDirectory())
    .toSorted()
    .map((topic) => join(top, topic, 'index.md'))
    .filter((path) => existsSync(path));
}

function headings(text: string): string[] {
  return text
    .split(NEWLINE)
    .filter((line) => line.startsWith('## '))
    .map((line) => line.slice(3).trim());
}

// One entry per document, and one per section of a document of the base. The library is searched
// by its index lines only: it holds thousands of files.
function indexEntries(root: string, index: string): Entry[] {
  return read(index)
    .split(NEWLINE)
    .flatMap((line) => {
      const match = INDEX_LINE.exec(line.trim());

      if (match === null) {
        return [];
      }
      const [
        ,
        title = '',
        path = '',
        summary = '',
      ] = match;
      const file = join(baseOf(root, path), path);
      const sections = path.startsWith('docs/') && existsSync(file) ? headings(read(file)) : [];

      return [
        {
          path,
          text: `${title}: ${summary}`,
        },
        ...sections.map((section) => ({
          path,
          section,
          text: `${title} ${section}`,
        })),
      ];
    });
}

function entries(root: string): Entry[] {
  const cached = cachedEntries.get(root);

  if (cached !== undefined) {
    return cached;
  }
  const found = [
    ...topicIndexes(root, 'docs'),
    ...topicIndexes(libraryBase(root), 'library'),
  ].flatMap((index) => indexEntries(root, index));

  cachedEntries.set(root, found);
  return found;
}

function words(text: string): string[] {
  return text
    .toLowerCase()
    .split(/[^a-z\d]+/)
    .filter((word) => word.length >= 2);
}

export function searchDocs(root: string, query: string): ToolResult {
  const asked = [...new Set(words(query))];

  if (asked.length === 0) {
    return {
      text: 'Give at least one English word to search.',
      isError: true,
    };
  }
  const found = entries(root)
    .map((entry) => {
      const known = new Set(words(entry.text));

      return {
        entry,
        score: asked.filter((word) => known.has(word)).length,
      };
    })
    .filter((item) => item.score > 0)
    .toSorted((first, second) => second.score - first.score || Number(first.entry.path.startsWith('library/')) - Number(second.entry.path.startsWith('library/')))
    .slice(0, MAX_RESULTS)
    .map(({ entry }) => entry.section === undefined ? `${entry.path} | ${entry.text}` : `${entry.path} | section: ${entry.section}`);

  return {
    text: found.length === 0 ? `No document matches "${query}". Try other English words.` : found.join(NEWLINE),
    isError: false,
  };
}

function safeDocument(root: string, path: string): string | undefined {
  if (!DOC_PATH.test(path) || path.endsWith('.fr.md') || path
    .split('/')
    .includes('..')) {
    return undefined;
  }
  const base = baseOf(root, path);
  const top = resolve(base, path.split('/')[0] ?? 'docs');
  const file = resolve(base, path);

  return relative(top, file).startsWith(`..${sep}`) || !existsSync(file) ? undefined : file;
}

function frontField(text: string, name: string): string {
  const match = new RegExp(`^${name}: (.+)$`, 'm').exec(text.split(/^---$/m)[1] ?? '');

  return match?.[1]?.trim() ?? '';
}

function tableOfContents(path: string, text: string): string {
  return [
    `${path}: ${frontField(text, 'title')}`,
    `Summary: ${frontField(text, 'summary')}`,
    'Sections:',
    ...headings(text).map((title, number) => /^\d+\. /.test(title) ? title : `${number + 1}. ${title}`),
    'Read one section with read_doc and its number or title.',
  ].join(NEWLINE);
}

function sectionBody(text: string, title: string): string {
  const lines = text.split(NEWLINE);
  const start = lines.indexOf(`## ${title}`);
  const end = lines.findIndex((line, index) => index > start && line.startsWith('## '));

  return lines
    .slice(start, end === -1 ? undefined : end)
    .join(NEWLINE)
    .trim();
}

function findSection(titles: string[], asked: string): string | undefined {
  const wanted = asked
    .trim()
    .toLowerCase();
  const number = Number.parseInt(wanted, 10);

  if (String(number) === wanted) {
    return titles[number - 1];
  }
  return titles.find((title) => title.toLowerCase() === wanted) ?? titles.find((title) => title.toLowerCase().includes(wanted));
}

// The sources a section cites, taken from the Sources section of its document.
function citedSources(text: string, body: string): string[] {
  const cited = new Set(body.match(/\[S\d+\]/g) ?? []);

  return sectionBody(text, 'Sources')
    .split(NEWLINE)
    .filter((line) => [...cited].some((tag) => line.includes(tag)));
}

function capped(text: string): string {
  return text.length <= MAX_CHARS ? text : `${text.slice(0, MAX_CHARS)}${NEWLINE}[truncated: the section is longer than ${MAX_CHARS} characters]`;
}

export function readDoc(root: string, path: string, section?: string): ToolResult {
  const file = safeDocument(root, path);

  if (file === undefined) {
    return {
      text: `No document "${path}" in docs/. Use a path that search_docs gives.`,
      isError: true,
    };
  }
  const text = read(file);

  if (section === undefined || section.trim() === '') {
    return {
      text: tableOfContents(path, text),
      isError: false,
    };
  }
  const title = findSection(headings(text), section);

  if (title === undefined) {
    return {
      text: `No section "${section}" in ${path}.${NEWLINE}${tableOfContents(path, text)}`,
      isError: true,
    };
  }
  const body = sectionBody(text, title);
  const sources = title === 'Sources' ? [] : citedSources(text, body);

  return {
    text: capped([
      body,
      ...sources.length === 0 ? [] : [
        '',
        'Sources:',
        ...sources,
      ],
    ].join(NEWLINE)),
    isError: false,
  };
}

function callTool(root: string, params: Json): ToolResult | undefined {
  const args = typeof params.arguments === 'object' && params.arguments !== null ? params.arguments as Json : {};
  const text = (name: string): string | undefined => typeof args[name] === 'string' ? args[name] : undefined;

  if (params.name === 'search_docs') {
    return searchDocs(root, text('query') ?? '');
  }
  if (params.name === 'read_doc') {
    return readDoc(root, text('path') ?? '', text('section'));
  }
  return undefined;
}

function requestVersion(params: Json | undefined): string | undefined {
  const meta = params?._meta;

  if (typeof meta !== 'object' || meta === null) {
    return undefined;
  }
  const version = (meta as Json)[VERSION_KEY];

  return typeof version === 'string' ? version : undefined;
}

function reply(id: Request['id'], result: Json): Json {
  return {
    jsonrpc: '2.0',
    id,
    result,
  };
}

function failure(id: Request['id'], code: number, message: string, data?: Json): Json {
  return {
    jsonrpc: '2.0',
    id,
    error: data === undefined ? {
      code,
      message,
    } : {
      code,
      message,
      data,
    },
  };
}

function initialize(request: Request): Json {
  const requested = request.params?.protocolVersion;
  const version = typeof requested === 'string' && LEGACY_VERSIONS.includes(requested) ? requested : LEGACY_VERSIONS[0];

  return reply(request.id, {
    protocolVersion: version,
    capabilities: { tools: {} },
    serverInfo: SERVER_INFO,
    instructions: INSTRUCTIONS,
  });
}

function discover(request: Request): Json {
  return reply(request.id, {
    resultType: 'complete',
    supportedVersions: [
      MODERN_VERSION,
      ...LEGACY_VERSIONS,
    ],
    capabilities: { tools: {} },
    _meta: { 'io.modelcontextprotocol/serverInfo': SERVER_INFO },
    instructions: INSTRUCTIONS,
  });
}

function toolCall(root: string, request: Request, modern: boolean): Json {
  const result = callTool(root, request.params ?? {});

  if (result === undefined) {
    return failure(request.id, -32602, `Unknown tool: ${String(request.params?.name)}`);
  }
  return reply(request.id, {
    ...modern ? { resultType: 'complete' } : {},
    content: [
      {
        type: 'text',
        text: result.text,
      },
    ],
    isError: result.isError,
  });
}

function operation(root: string, request: Request, modern: boolean): Json {
  switch (request.method) {
    case 'ping': {
      return reply(request.id, {});
    }
    case 'tools/list': {
      return reply(request.id, {
        ...modern ? { resultType: 'complete' } : {},
        tools: TOOLS,
      });
    }
    case 'tools/call': {
      return toolCall(root, request, modern);
    }
    default: {
      return failure(request.id, -32601, `Method not found: ${String(request.method)}`);
    }
  }
}

// Answers one request, or returns undefined for a notification.
export function handle(root: string, request: Request): Json | undefined {
  if (request.id === undefined || request.id === null) {
    return undefined;
  }
  if (request.method === 'initialize') {
    return initialize(request);
  }
  const version = requestVersion(request.params);

  if (version !== undefined && version !== MODERN_VERSION) {
    return failure(request.id, -32022, 'Unsupported protocol version', {
      supported: [
        MODERN_VERSION,
        ...LEGACY_VERSIONS,
      ],
      requested: version,
    });
  }
  if (request.method === 'server/discover') {
    return discover(request);
  }
  return operation(root, request, version !== undefined);
}

export function handleLine(root: string, line: string): Json | undefined {
  if (line.trim() === '') {
    return undefined;
  }
  try {
    const request = JSON.parse(line) as unknown;

    if (typeof request !== 'object' || request === null || Array.isArray(request)) {
      return failure(null, -32600, 'Invalid request');
    }
    return handle(root, request as Request);
  } catch {
    return failure(null, -32700, 'Parse error');
  }
}

function main(): void {
  const { values } = parseArgs({ options: { root: { type: 'string' } } });
  const root = resolveDocsRoot(resolve(values.root ?? process.cwd()));
  const lines = createInterface({ input: process.stdin });

  process.stderr.write(`docs server: reading ${join(root, 'docs')}${NEWLINE}`);
  lines.on('line', (line) => {
    const answer = handleLine(root, line);

    if (answer !== undefined) {
      process.stdout.write(`${JSON.stringify(answer)}${NEWLINE}`);
    }
  });
}

if (process.argv[1] !== undefined && realpathSync(process.argv[1]) === realpathSync(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url))) {
  main();
}

# Knowledge tools

- Call `query_knowledge_files` with the question of the user before you answer.
- If it finds nothing, call it again with one plain keyword. Then try a second keyword.
- To read a whole document, call `view_knowledge_file` with the file id of a result.
- Name each document you used, by its title, on a last line that starts with `Sources:`.

=====BASE-EXPORT-1-571e463b6744===== FILE 1/1 aedd1508d498e3b08ee6cbb1af5d32eca00e6a638f048dbcd72c66b9bb89399c RAW adapters/open-webui/push.ts
// Pushes the chat profile to Open WebUI through its REST API: knowledge bases, skills and models,
// each shared for reading as OPEN_WEBUI_SHARE says. Written for Open WebUI 0.13.3.
// Run from the base root, after the export wrote chat/ and adapters/open-webui/manifest.json:
//   node adapters/open-webui/push.ts [--dry-run]
// Node 22.18 or later. No build step. No npm package.
//
// Environment:
//   OPEN_WEBUI_URL         Base address of the instance.
//   OPEN_WEBUI_TOKEN       API key or session token. Never written to a file.
//   OPEN_WEBUI_BASE_MODEL  Id of the base model every custom model wraps.
//   OPEN_WEBUI_SHARE       "private", "public" or "group:<group name>". Default: private.
//   OPEN_WEBUI_MODE        "native" or "legacy". Default: the mode of the manifest.
//   OPEN_WEBUI_LIBRARY     Optional: tools of library/ to push as knowledge bases, "core", "all"
//                          or a comma list. Each goes to the chat model its source names.
import { createHash } from 'node:crypto';
import {
  existsSync,
  readdirSync,
  readFileSync,
  statSync,
  writeFileSync,
} from 'node:fs';
import {
  basename,
  dirname,
  join,
  relative,
} from 'node:path';
import { pathToFileURL } from 'node:url';

type Mode = 'native' | 'legacy';

interface Grant {
  principal_type: 'user' | 'group';
  principal_id: string;
  permission: 'read' | 'write';
}

interface KnowledgeEntry {
  key: string;
  name: string;
  description: string;
  dir: string;
}

interface SkillEntry {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  description: string;
  dir: string;
}

interface ModelEntry {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  description: string;
  prompt: string;
  knowledge: string[];
  skills: string[];
}

interface Manifest {
  mode: Mode;
  toolsBlock: string;
  knowledge: KnowledgeEntry[];
  skills: SkillEntry[];
  models: ModelEntry[];
}

interface State {
  knowledge: Record<string, string>;
}

interface LocalFile {
  data: Buffer;
  filename: string;
  path: string;
  checksum: string;
  size: number;
}

interface SyncDiff {
  added: {
    filename: string;
    path: string;
  }[];
  modified: {
    filename: string;
    path: string;
    stale_file_id: string;
  }[];
  deleted: { file_id: string }[];
  mkdir: string[];
  rmdir: string[];
  directory_map: Record<string, string>;
}

export interface PushConfig {
  url: string;
  token: string;
  baseModel: string;
  share: string;
  mode?: Mode;
  root: string;
  // Tools of library/ to push: "core", "all" or a comma list.
  library?: string;
  dryRun: boolean;
  log: (line: string) => void;
  pollMs?: number;
}

type Call = (method: string, path: string, body?: unknown, form?: FormData) => Promise<unknown>;

class ApiError extends Error {
  readonly status: number;

  constructor(message: string, status: number) {
    super(message);
    this.status = status;
  }
}

const OFF_TOOLS = [
  'time',
  'user_input',
  'memory',
  'chats',
  'notes',
  'files',
  'channels',
  'notifications',
  'web_search',
  'image_generation',
  'code_interpreter',
  'tasks',
  'automations',
  'calendar',
  'subagents',
  'skills',
  'tools',
];

function record(value: unknown): Record<string, unknown> {
  return typeof value === 'object' && value !== null ? Object.fromEntries(Object.entries(value)) : {};
}

function text(value: unknown): string {
  return typeof value === 'string' ? value : '';
}

function createCall(config: PushConfig): Call {
  const base = config.url.replace(/\/+$/, '');

  return async (method, path, body, form) => {
    if (config.dryRun && method !== 'GET') {
      config.log(`dry-run ${method} ${path}`);
      return {};
    }
    const headers: Record<string, string> = { Authorization: `Bearer ${config.token}` };

    if (form === undefined && body !== undefined) {
      headers['Content-Type'] = 'application/json';
    }
    const response = await fetch(base + path, {
      method,
      headers,
      body: form ?? (body === undefined ? undefined : JSON.stringify(body)),
    });
    const answer = await response.text();

    if (!response.ok) {
      throw new ApiError(`${method} ${path} -> ${String(response.status)} ${answer.slice(0, 300)}`, response.status);
    }
    return answer === '' ? null : JSON.parse(answer) as unknown;
  };
}

function walk(folder: string): string[] {
  return readdirSync(folder)
    .toSorted()
    .filter((entry) => !entry.startsWith('.'))
    .flatMap((entry) => {
      const full = join(folder, entry);

      return statSync(full).isDirectory() ? walk(full) : [full];
    });
}

function posixRelative(from: string, to: string): string {
  return relative(from, to)
    .split('\\')
    .join('/');
}

async function resolveGrants(call: Call, share: string): Promise<Grant[]> {
  if (share === 'private') {
    return [];
  }
  if (share === 'public') {
    return [
      {
        principal_type: 'user',
        principal_id: '*',
        permission: 'read',
      },
    ];
  }
  if (!share.startsWith('group:')) {
    throw new Error(`OPEN_WEBUI_SHARE must be private, public or group:<name>, got ${share}`);
  }
  const wanted = share.slice('group:'.length);
  const groups = await call('GET', '/api/v1/groups/');
  const group = (Array.isArray(groups) ? groups : [])
    .map(record)
    .find((item) => item['name'] === wanted);

  if (group === undefined) {
    throw new Error(`Group not found: ${wanted}`);
  }
  return [
    {
      principal_type: 'group',
      principal_id: text(group['id']),
      permission: 'read',
    },
  ];
}

async function existingSkillVersion(call: Call, id: string): Promise<string> {
  try {
    return text(record(await call('GET', `/api/v1/skills/id/${id}`))['version_id']);
  } catch (error) {
    if (error instanceof ApiError && error.status === 404) {
      return '';
    }
    throw error;
  }
}

async function pushSkill(call: Call, config: PushConfig, skill: SkillEntry, grants: Grant[]): Promise<void> {
  const folder = join(config.root, skill.dir);
  const files = walk(folder).map((file) => {
    return {
      path: posixRelative(folder, file),
      content: readFileSync(file, 'utf8'),
    };
  });
  const form = {
    id: skill.id,
    name: skill.name,
    description: skill.description,
    files,
    meta: { tags: [] },
    is_active: true,
  };
  const version = await existingSkillVersion(call, skill.id);

  if (version === '') {
    await call('POST', '/api/v1/skills/create', {
      ...form,
      access_grants: grants,
    });
  } else {
    await call('POST', `/api/v1/skills/id/${skill.id}/update`, {
      ...form,
      expected_version_id: version,
    });
  }
  await call('POST', `/api/v1/skills/id/${skill.id}/access/update`, { access_grants: grants });
  config.log(`skill ${skill.id}: ${String(files.length)} file(s)`);
}

function localFiles(folder: string): LocalFile[] {
  return walk(folder).map((file) => {
    const data = readFileSync(file);
    const path = dirname(posixRelative(folder, file));

    return {
      data,
      filename: basename(file),
      path: path === '.' ? '' : path,
      checksum: createHash('sha256')
        .update(data)
        .digest('hex'),
      size: data.length,
    };
  });
}

async function knowledgeId(call: Call, config: PushConfig, entry: KnowledgeEntry, grants: Grant[], state: State): Promise<string> {
  const known = state.knowledge[entry.key] ?? '';

  if (known !== '') {
    try {
      await call('GET', `/api/v1/knowledge/${known}`);
      return known;
    } catch (error) {
      if (!(error instanceof ApiError) || error.status !== 404) {
        throw error;
      }
    }
  }
  const created = record(await call('POST', '/api/v1/knowledge/create', {
    name: entry.name,
    description: entry.description,
    access_grants: grants,
  }));
  const id = text(created['id']) || `dry-run:${entry.key}`;

  state.knowledge[entry.key] = id;
  return id;
}

async function syncDiff(call: Call, config: PushConfig, id: string, local: LocalFile[]): Promise<SyncDiff> {
  if (config.dryRun) {
    return {
      added: local,
      modified: [],
      deleted: [],
      mkdir: [],
      rmdir: [],
      directory_map: {},
    };
  }
  const manifest = local.map((file) => {
    return {
      filename: file.filename,
      path: file.path,
      checksum: file.checksum,
      size: file.size,
    };
  });

  return await call('POST', `/api/v1/knowledge/${id}/sync/diff`, { manifest }) as SyncDiff;
}

async function waitForFile(call: Call, config: PushConfig, fileId: string): Promise<void> {
  for (let attempt = 0; attempt < 300; attempt += 1) {
    const status = text(record(await call('GET', `/api/v1/files/${fileId}/process/status`))['status']);

    if (status === 'completed') {
      return;
    }
    if (status === 'failed') {
      throw new Error(`File processing failed: ${fileId}`);
    }
    await new Promise((resolve) => {
      setTimeout(resolve, config.pollMs ?? 1000);
    });
  }
  throw new Error(`File processing timed out: ${fileId}`);
}

async function makeDirectories(call: Call, id: string, paths: string[], directories: Record<string, string>): Promise<void> {
  for (const path of paths) {
    const parent = dirname(path) === '.' ? null : directories[dirname(path)] ?? null;
    const created = record(await call('POST', `/api/v1/knowledge/${id}/dirs/create`, {
      name: basename(path),
      parent_id: parent,
    }));

    directories[path] = text(created['id']) || `dry-run:${path}`;
  }
}

async function upload(call: Call, config: PushConfig, id: string, file: LocalFile, directories: Record<string, string>): Promise<void> {
  const metadata: Record<string, string> = {
    knowledge_id: id,
    file_hash: file.checksum,
  };

  if (file.path !== '') {
    metadata['directory_id'] = directories[file.path] ?? '';
  }
  const form = new FormData();

  form.append('file', new Blob([file.data], { type: 'text/markdown' }), file.filename);
  form.append('metadata', JSON.stringify(metadata));
  const uploaded = record(await call('POST', '/api/v1/files/?process=true&process_in_background=false', undefined, form));

  if (!config.dryRun) {
    await waitForFile(call, config, text(uploaded['id']));
  }
}

async function pushKnowledge(call: Call, config: PushConfig, entry: KnowledgeEntry, grants: Grant[], state: State): Promise<string> {
  const id = await knowledgeId(call, config, entry, grants, state);
  const local = localFiles(join(config.root, entry.dir));
  const diff = await syncDiff(call, config, id, local);
  const directories = { ...diff.directory_map };
  const changed = [
    ...diff.added,
    ...diff.modified,
  ];
  const stale = [
    ...diff.deleted.map((item) => item.file_id),
    ...diff.modified.map((item) => item.stale_file_id),
  ];

  await makeDirectories(call, id, diff.mkdir, directories);
  for (const item of changed) {
    const file = local.find((candidate) => candidate.filename === item.filename && candidate.path === item.path);

    if (file !== undefined) {
      await upload(call, config, id, file, directories);
    }
  }
  if (stale.length > 0 || diff.rmdir.length > 0) {
    await call('POST', `/api/v1/knowledge/${id}/sync/cleanup`, {
      file_ids: stale,
      dir_ids: diff.rmdir,
    });
  }
  await call('POST', `/api/v1/knowledge/${id}/access/update`, { access_grants: grants });
  config.log(`knowledge ${entry.key}: ${String(changed.length)} uploaded, ${String(stale.length)} removed, ${String(local.length)} local`);
  return id;
}

function systemPrompt(config: PushConfig, entry: ModelEntry, manifest: Manifest, mode: Mode): string {
  const prompt = readFileSync(join(config.root, entry.prompt), 'utf8')
    .replace(/^---\n[\s\S]*?\n---\n/, '')
    .trim();

  if (mode === 'native' && entry.knowledge.length > 0) {
    // The block is a document of its own, with a title; in the prompt it becomes one more section.
    const tools = readFileSync(join(config.root, manifest.toolsBlock), 'utf8')
      .trim()
      .replace(/^# /, '## ');

    return `${prompt}\n\n${tools}`;
  }
  return prompt;
}

interface ModelContext {
  config: PushConfig;
  manifest: Manifest;
  mode: Mode;
  ids: Record<string, string>;
  grants: Grant[];
}

function modelKnowledge(context: ModelContext, entry: ModelEntry): Record<string, string>[] {
  return entry.knowledge.map((key) => {
    const source = context.manifest.knowledge.find((item) => item.key === key);
    const id = context.ids[key];

    if (source === undefined || id === undefined) {
      throw new Error(`Unknown knowledge ${key} in model ${entry.id}`);
    }
    return {
      id,
      name: source.name,
      description: source.description,
      type: 'collection',
    };
  });
}

// Every built-in tool is off, except the knowledge tools in native mode for a model with knowledge.
function capabilities(usesKnowledgeTools: boolean): Record<string, unknown> {
  return {
    capabilities: {
      vision: false,
      file_upload: true,
      file_context: true,
      web_search: false,
      image_generation: false,
      code_interpreter: false,
      terminal: false,
      citations: true,
      status_updates: true,
      memory: false,
      builtin_tools: usesKnowledgeTools,
    },
    builtinTools: Object.fromEntries([
      ['knowledge', usesKnowledgeTools],
      ...OFF_TOOLS.map((key) => [
        key,
        false,
      ]),
    ]),
  };
}

export function buildModel(context: ModelContext, entry: ModelEntry): Record<string, unknown> {
  return {
    id: entry.id,
    name: entry.name,
    base_model_id: context.config.baseModel,
    is_active: true,
    params: {
      system: systemPrompt(context.config, entry, context.manifest, context.mode),
      function_calling: context.mode,
    },
    meta: {
      description: entry.description,
      ...capabilities(context.mode === 'native' && entry.knowledge.length > 0),
      knowledge: modelKnowledge(context, entry),
      skillIds: entry.skills,
      toolIds: [],
      defaultFeatureIds: [],
    },
    access_grants: context.grants,
  };
}

interface LibrarySource {
  tool: string;
  title?: string;
  core?: boolean;
  chat?: string;
}

function librarySources(root: string, selection: string): LibrarySource[] {
  const path = join(root, 'library', 'sources.json');

  if (!existsSync(path)) {
    throw new Error('OPEN_WEBUI_LIBRARY is set, but library/sources.json is missing: import the library parts first.');
  }
  const all = (JSON.parse(readFileSync(path, 'utf8')) as { sources: LibrarySource[] }).sources;

  if (selection === 'all' || selection === 'core') {
    return all.filter((source) => selection === 'all' || source.core === true);
  }
  return selection
    .split(',')
    .map((tool) => {
      const found = all.find((source) => source.tool === tool.trim());

      if (found === undefined) {
        throw new Error(`OPEN_WEBUI_LIBRARY: unknown tool ${tool.trim()}`);
      }
      return found;
    });
}

// The manifest with one knowledge base per chosen tool of library/, attached to the model of its source.
export function withLibrary(manifest: Manifest, root: string, selection?: string): Manifest {
  if (selection === undefined || selection.trim() === '') {
    return manifest;
  }
  const chosen = librarySources(root, selection).filter((source) => {
    return source.chat !== undefined && existsSync(join(root, 'library', source.tool));
  });
  const knowledge = chosen.map((source) => {
    return {
      key: `library-${source.tool}`,
      name: `${source.tool} official docs`,
      description: `${source.title ?? source.tool}: official documentation, one file per page.`,
      dir: `library/${source.tool}`,
    };
  });

  return {
    ...manifest,
    knowledge: [
      ...manifest.knowledge,
      ...knowledge,
    ],
    models: manifest.models.map((model) => {
      return {
        ...model,
        knowledge: [
          ...model.knowledge,
          ...chosen
            .filter((source) => source.chat === model.id)
            .map((source) => `library-${source.tool}`),
        ],
      };
    }),
  };
}

async function checkInstance(call: Call, config: PushConfig, mode: Mode): Promise<void> {
  const version = text(record(await call('GET', '/api/version'))['version']);

  config.log(`Open WebUI ${version}, mode ${mode}, share ${config.share}${config.dryRun ? ', dry run' : ''}`);
  const listed = record(await call('GET', '/api/models'))['data'];
  const ids = (Array.isArray(listed) ? listed : []).map((model) => text(record(model)['id']));

  if (!ids.includes(config.baseModel)) {
    throw new Error('Base model not found. Check OPEN_WEBUI_BASE_MODEL.');
  }
}

export async function push(config: PushConfig): Promise<void> {
  const adapter = join(config.root, 'adapters', 'open-webui');
  const stateFile = join(adapter, 'state.local.json');
  const manifest = withLibrary(JSON.parse(readFileSync(join(adapter, 'manifest.json'), 'utf8')) as Manifest, config.root, config.library);
  const mode = config.mode ?? manifest.mode;
  const call = createCall(config);
  const state = existsSync(stateFile) ? JSON.parse(readFileSync(stateFile, 'utf8')) as State : { knowledge: {} };

  await checkInstance(call, config, mode);
  const grants = await resolveGrants(call, config.share);

  for (const skill of manifest.skills) {
    await pushSkill(call, config, skill, grants);
  }
  const ids: Record<string, string> = {};

  for (const entry of manifest.knowledge) {
    ids[entry.key] = await pushKnowledge(call, config, entry, grants, state);
  }
  if (!config.dryRun) {
    writeFileSync(stateFile, `${JSON.stringify(state, null, 2)}\n`);
  }
  const context = {
    config,
    manifest,
    mode,
    ids,
    grants,
  };
  const models = manifest.models.map((entry) => buildModel(context, entry));

  await call('POST', '/api/v1/models/import', { models });
  for (const model of models) {
    await call('POST', '/api/v1/models/model/access/update', {
      id: model['id'],
      access_grants: grants,
    });
    config.log(`model ${text(model['id'])}`);
  }
  config.log('Done.');
}

function environment(name: string, fallback?: string): string {
  const value = process.env[name] ?? fallback ?? '';

  if (value === '') {
    throw new Error(`Missing environment variable ${name}`);
  }
  return value;
}

function modeFromEnvironment(): Mode | undefined {
  const value = process.env['OPEN_WEBUI_MODE'] ?? '';

  if (value === '') {
    return undefined;
  }
  if (value !== 'native' && value !== 'legacy') {
    throw new Error('OPEN_WEBUI_MODE must be native or legacy');
  }
  return value;
}

async function main(): Promise<void> {
  await push({
    url: environment('OPEN_WEBUI_URL'),
    token: environment('OPEN_WEBUI_TOKEN'),
    baseModel: environment('OPEN_WEBUI_BASE_MODEL'),
    share: environment('OPEN_WEBUI_SHARE', 'private'),
    mode: modeFromEnvironment(),
    root: process.cwd(),
    ...process.env['OPEN_WEBUI_LIBRARY'] === undefined ? {} : { library: process.env['OPEN_WEBUI_LIBRARY'] },
    dryRun: process.argv.includes('--dry-run'),
    log: (line) => {
      process.stdout.write(`${line}\n`);
    },
  });
}

if (process.argv[1] !== undefined && import.meta.url === pathToFileURL(process.argv[1]).href) {
  main().catch((error: unknown) => {
    process.stderr.write(`${error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error)}\n`);
    process.exitCode = 1;
  });
}
